```ts
interface ActionsContext {
    selectedActionId?: EARS.EntityId;
    /**
     * The call the outstanding `ACTION_SELECT` was asked under, or absent when nothing is outstanding.
     *
     * `selectedActionId` cannot do this job: it is written *from* the answer, so until one lands there is
     * nothing to compare an arriving one against, and two quick selections are decided by whichever answer
     * arrives last.
     *
     * **A domain id cannot do this job, for two independent reasons.** It does not distinguish windows, so
     * another window's answer for the same action satisfies it; and it does not distinguish asks, so a slot
     * holding one is only ever overwritten, never emptied — which names the last ask for the life of the
     * surface. A call is per ask and per window, and the slot is cleared when its answer lands.
     */
    pendingActionCall: string | null;
    actions: ActionEntity[];
    selectedAction?: ActionEntity;
    totalCount: number;
    page: number;
    totalPages: number;
    loadingMore: boolean;
    categories: Category[];
    selectedCategories: string[];
    actionsImport: {
        status: 'error' | 'idle' | 'importing' | 'success';
        errors: string[];
        importedCount: number;
    };
    actionsExport: {
        status: 'error' | 'exporting' | 'idle' | 'success';
        errors: string[];
        filePath: string;
        actionCount: number;
    };
    formData: {
        label: string;
        description?: string;
        category?: string;
        input: Record<string, ActionParameter>;
        actionFn: string;
        output?: any;
        parametersExpanded?: boolean;
        outputExpanded?: boolean;
        metadataExpanded?: boolean;
    };
}

/**
 * `claude auth` — login, logout, status.
 *
 * `login` is interactive by nature (opens a browser for Claude.ai, prompts
 * for SSO, etc.); we expose it anyway for completeness but callers should
 * usually delegate to the CLI UI rather than call this programmatically.
 */

/** Shape of `claude auth status --json` — passthrough, CLI may add fields. */

    apiProvider?: string;

}

/**
 * Block-based interaction helpers for creating composable messages
 *
 * These helpers make it easy to create messages using reusable blocks that can be
 * mixed and matched to create complex interactions.
 */
interface BlockMessageBase {
    threadId: EARS.EntityId;
    text: string;
    blocks: BlockConfig[];
    forkable?: boolean;
}

type BlockMessageOptions = BlockMessageBase & AutoHideOptions;

/**
 * Shapes a block can emit back to the backend when the user interacts
 * with it. Non-discriminated on purpose — text and choice blocks emit
 * raw primitives on submit (TextInput.vue:207, ChoiceInput.vue:225),
 * while approval and cancel blocks emit tagged objects
 * (InteractionContainer.vue:156-167). Wrapping the primitives into
 * `{ type: 'text', value: string }` etc. would be a wire-shape break,
 * so we encode the reality instead: a union of every observed shape
 * with no synthetic discriminator.
 *
 * Consumers MUST narrow before using the value, and each narrows where it
 * reads it rather than through a shared parser. Approval is the worked
 * example: `CC: Route Response` (content/actions/claude-code/route-response.ts)
 * counts a response as a deny only when it says so —
 * `approved === false || cancelled === true` — having already established
 * that it answers the pending control request it is routing.
 *
 * When adding a new block type, extend this union first and narrow at the
 * site that reads it.
 *
 * `blockResponse?: unknown` at the storage boundary is more defensive than
 * assuming the union is exhaustive; the event-level and field-level types use
 * the union, because every emit matches one of its arms.
 */
type BlockResponse = {
    nativeQuestionAnswer: ClaudeQuestionAnswer;
    nativeQuestionRenderToken?: string;
    nativeQuestionDraftStarted?: boolean;
}
/** Approval buttons: InteractionContainer `handleApprove`/`handleDeny`. */
 | {
    approved: boolean;
    reason?: string;
}
/** Cancel path: InteractionContainer `handleCancel`. */
 | {
    cancelled: true;
}
/** Text input (single or multiline) and single-select choice emit a raw string. */
 | string
/** Multi-select choice emits a raw string array (of choice ids). */
 | string[];

interface ClaudeConversation {
    readonly handle: QueryHandle;
    readonly busy: boolean;
    readonly closing: boolean;
    readonly sessionId?: string;
    readonly sessionCwd?: string;
    /** Last acknowledged selection, distinct from observed effective model. */
    readonly modelSelection?: string;
    readonly done: Promise<void>;
    start(input: string | UserInputMessage, consume: ClaudeTurnConsumer): string;
    release(handle: QueryHandle): boolean;
    ownsTurn(handle: QueryHandle): boolean;
    subscribe(listener: (event: StreamLine) => void): () => void;
    close(): Promise<void>;
    kill(): void;
}

interface ClaudeConversationTurn {
    handle: QueryHandle;
    inputId?: string;
    /** Explicit teardown won before or during consumption; never register new app ownership. */
    stopped: boolean;
    /** Native process cost already accounted for before this turn. */
    costBaseline: number;
    activity: ClaudeNestedActivity;
}

interface ClaudeNativeSessionMetadata {
    cwd: string;
    model: string;
    turns: number;
    startedAt: number;
    totalCostUsd?: number;
    permissionMode?: PermissionMode;
}

interface ClaudeNativeSubmissionReceipt {
    sessionId: string;
    uuid: string;
    text: string;
}

type ClaudeNativeTerminalHandoffOptions = Pick<ClaudeNativeTerminalOptions, 'cliPath' | 'columns' | 'env' | 'rows'>;

interface ClaudeNativeTerminalOptions {
    sessionId: string;
    cwd: string;
    settings: ClaudeSettings;
    permissionMode: PermissionMode;
    cliPath?: string;
    env?: NodeJS.ProcessEnv;
    columns?: number;
    rows?: number;
    model?: string;
    allowedTools?: string[];
    disallowedTools?: string[];
    appendSystemPrompt?: string;
    addDir?: string[];
    pluginDir?: string[];
    signal?: AbortSignal;
}

interface ClaudeNativeTerminalOwner {
    /** Reviewed native UI operations; reverse hosts and the full UI owner are integrated separately. */
    readonly uiTransport: ClaudeTerminalUiTransport;
    readonly state: ClaudeNativeTerminalState;
    readonly busy: boolean;
    readonly modelSelection?: string;
    readonly done: Promise<void>;
    /** Register persistence work before the process exits; done waits for these callbacks. */
    beforeRelease(listener: () => Promise<void>): void;
    /** Raw native terminal bytes, sequenced independently of presentation metadata. */
    readonly output: readonly {
        sequence: number;
        text: string;
    }[];
    readJournal(): Promise<ClaudeNativeJournalBatch>;
    readonly records: readonly {
        sequence: number;
        record: ClaudeNativeTerminalRecord;
    }[];
    subscribe(listener: () => void): () => void;
    input(ownerId: string, text: string): boolean;
    resize(ownerId: string, columns: number, rows: number): boolean;
    holdQuestionDialog(request: ClaudeNativeQuestionHoldRequest): Promise<void>;
    renderQuestionDialog(request: ClaudeNativeQuestionRenderRequest): Promise<ClaudeNativeQuestionRender>;
    getQuestionDialogs(): Promise<ClaudeNativeQuestionDialog[]>;
    answerQuestionDialog(response: ClaudeNativeQuestionDialogResponse): Promise<void>;
    getBashDialogs(): Promise<ClaudeNativeBashDialog[]>;
    answerBashDialog(response: ClaudeNativeBashDialogResponse): Promise<void>;
    getPlanDialogs(): Promise<ClaudeNativePlanDialog[]>;
    answerPlanDialog(response: ClaudeNativePlanDialogResponse): Promise<void>;
    getFileDialogs(): Promise<ClaudeNativeFileDialog[]>;
    answerFileDialog(response: ClaudeNativeFileDialogResponse): Promise<void>;
    getMcpStatus(): Promise<ClaudeMcpStatus>;
    controlMcpServer(serverName: string, operation: 'disable' | 'enable' | 'reconnect'): Promise<ClaudeMcpStatus>;
    getModels(options?: {
        includeUnsupportedModels?: boolean;
    }): Promise<ClaudeModelCatalog>;
    renameSession(title: string): Promise<{
        title: string;
        message: string;
    }>;
    setModel(model: string): Promise<string>;
    setPermissionMode(mode: PermissionMode): Promise<PermissionMode>;
    submit(text: string, images?: readonly ClaudeNativeImage[]): Promise<ClaudeNativeSubmissionReceipt>;
    close(): Promise<void>;
    kill(): void;
}

interface ClaudeNativeTerminalRecord {
    sessionId: string;
    uuid: string;
    door: string;
    origin: Record<string, unknown>;
    message: {
        type: 'assistant' | 'attachment' | 'system' | 'user';
        role?: 'assistant' | 'user';
        isMeta?: true;
        content: {
            type: string;
            [key: string]: unknown;
        }[];
    };
}

interface ClaudeNativeTerminalState {
    ownerId: string;
    sessionId: string;
    cwd: string;
    status: 'error' | 'exited' | 'live' | 'starting';
    /** Process attached to the PTY; may be a configured launcher. */
    pid?: number;
    /** Authenticated native executable beneath that launcher. */
    nativePid?: number;
    exitCode?: number;
    busy: boolean;
    lastTurn?: {
        id: string;
        reason: 'aborted' | 'answer' | 'error' | 'refusal';
    };
    session?: ClaudeNativeSessionMetadata;
    mainStatus?: string;
    tasks: ClaudeNativeTerminalTask[];
    problem?: string;
}

/** Native task ids and terminal owners are independent of apack thread ids. */
interface ClaudeNativeTerminalTask {
    id: string;
    name: string;
    status: string;
    statusText?: string;
}

interface ClaudeNestedAccounting {
    toolCallCount: number;
    mutatedPaths: string[];
    hadErrors: boolean;
}

interface ClaudeNestedActivity {
    /** Bind an observed root tool to its existing app activity, for this process lifetime. */
    register(rootToolId: string, receive: (event: StreamLine) => void): void;
    /** Drain facts once, at the next native result; output bindings remain registered. */
    takeAccounting(): ClaudeNestedAccounting;
}

type ClaudePlan = z.infer<typeof ClaudePlanSchema>;

/** Native lookup does not create a plan; paths can be omitted by the native access mode. */
declare const ClaudePlanSchema: z.ZodDiscriminatedUnion<"exists", [z.ZodObject<{
    exists: z.ZodLiteral<false>;
}, "passthrough", z.ZodTypeAny, z.objectOutputType<{
    exists: z.ZodLiteral<false>;
}, z.ZodTypeAny, "passthrough">, z.objectInputType<{
    exists: z.ZodLiteral<false>;
}, z.ZodTypeAny, "passthrough">>, z.ZodObject<{
    exists: z.ZodLiteral<true>;
    content: z.ZodString;
    path: z.ZodOptional<z.ZodString>;
}, "passthrough", z.ZodTypeAny, z.objectOutputType<{
    exists: z.ZodLiteral<true>;
    content: z.ZodString;
    path: z.ZodOptional<z.ZodString>;
}, z.ZodTypeAny, "passthrough">, z.objectInputType<{
    exists: z.ZodLiteral<true>;
    content: z.ZodString;
    path: z.ZodOptional<z.ZodString>;
}, z.ZodTypeAny, "passthrough">>]>;

type ClaudePromptCompletionReply = {
    revision: number;
    status: 'ready';
    result: ClaudeUiPromptCompletions;
} | {
    revision: number;
    status: 'error';
    message: string;
};

type ClaudePromptCompletionRequest = Omit<ClaudeUiPromptAutocomplete, 'client_id' | 'surface'> & {
    revision: number;
};

type ClaudePromptEdit = Omit<ClaudeUiPromptEdit, 'client_id' | 'surface'> & {
    revision: number;
};

interface ClaudePromptHost {
    read(signal: AbortSignal, expiresAt: number): Promise<ClaudePromptHostBox | undefined>;
    fill(input: ClaudePromptHostFill, signal: AbortSignal, expiresAt: number): Promise<boolean>;
    suggest?(text: string, signal: AbortSignal, expiresAt: number): Promise<boolean>;
}

type ClaudePromptHostBox = z.infer<typeof box>;

type ClaudePromptHostFill = z.infer<typeof fill>;

type ClaudePromptHostRequest = {
    requestId: string;
    revision: number;
    expiresAt: number;
} & ({
    kind: 'read';
} | {
    kind: 'fill';
    input: ClaudePromptHostFill;
} | {
    kind: 'suggest';
    text: string;
});

type ClaudePromptHostResponse = {
    requestId: string;
} & ({
    kind: 'read';
    box?: ClaudePromptHostBox;
} | {
    kind: 'fill';
    filled: boolean;
} | {
    kind: 'suggest';
    shown: boolean;
});

type ClaudePromptReply = {
    revision: number;
    status: 'applied';
    frame: ClaudeUiPromptFrame;
} | {
    revision: number;
    status: 'error';
    message: string;
};

interface ClaudeQuestionAnswer {
    answers?: Record<string, string | string[]>;
    annotations?: Record<string, {
        preview?: string;
        notes?: string;
    }>;
    response?: string;
    followUp?: boolean;
}

interface ClaudeSessionContextOptions extends Pick<QueryOptions, 'cliPath' | 'env' | 'signal'> {
    sessionId: string;
    cwd: string;
}

interface ClaudeSessionFileRewindOptions extends Pick<QueryOptions, 'cliPath' | 'env' | 'signal'> {
    sessionId: string;
    userMessageId: string;
    cwd: string;
}

interface ClaudeSessionPlanOptions extends Pick<QueryOptions, 'cliPath' | 'env' | 'signal'> {
    sessionId: string;
    cwd: string;
}

interface ClaudeSessionResumeTargetOptions extends Pick<QueryOptions, 'cliPath' | 'env' | 'signal'> {
    sessionId: string;
    messageUuid: string;
    cwd: string;
}

interface ClaudeTerminalSubmissionState {
    ownerId: string;
    revision: number;
    status: 'accepted' | 'pending' | 'rejected';
    receipt?: ClaudeNativeSubmissionReceipt;
    problem?: string;
}

interface ClaudeTerminalUiTransport {
    readonly promptHosts: Pick<ReturnType<typeof createClaudePromptHosts>, 'answers' | 'detach' | 'register'>;
    request(value: Record<string, unknown> & {
        subtype: string;
    }): Promise<unknown>;
    subscribe(listener: (event: Record<string, unknown>) => void): () => void;
}

type ClaudeTerminalViewReply = ClaudeTerminalViewSnapshot & {
    type: 'CLAUDE_TERMINAL_STATE';
    threadId: string;
    mountId: string;
};

type ClaudeTerminalViewRequest = {
    threadId: string;
    mountId: string;
} & ({
    type: 'CLAUDE_TERMINAL_MOUNT';
} | {
    type: 'CLAUDE_TERMINAL_UNMOUNT';
} | {
    type: 'CLAUDE_TERMINAL_OPEN';
} | {
    type: 'CLAUDE_TERMINAL_SUBMIT';
    ownerId: string;
    revision: number;
    text: string;
    references?: MessageReferences;
} | {
    type: 'CLAUDE_TERMINAL_INPUT';
    ownerId: string;
    text: string;
} | {
    type: 'CLAUDE_TERMINAL_RESIZE';
    ownerId: string;
    columns: number;
    rows: number;
} | {
    type: 'CLAUDE_TERMINAL_CLOSE';
    ownerId: string;
});

type ClaudeTerminalViewSnapshot = {
    submission?: ClaudeTerminalSubmissionState;
    state?: ClaudeNativeTerminalState;
    opening: boolean;
    problem?: string;
    output: {
        sequence: number;
        text: string;
    }[];
};

type ClaudeTurnConsumer = (turn: ClaudeConversationTurn) => Promise<void>;

type ClaudeUiAttach = z.infer<typeof attach>;

interface ClaudeUiBoxLayout {
    position?: 'absolute' | 'relative';
    top?: number;
    bottom?: number;
    left?: number;
    right?: number;
    flexDirection?: 'column' | 'column-reverse' | 'row' | 'row-reverse';
    flexGrow?: number;
    flexShrink?: number;
    flexWrap?: 'nowrap' | 'wrap' | 'wrap-reverse';
    alignItems?: 'center' | 'flex-end' | 'flex-start' | 'stretch';
    alignSelf?: 'auto' | 'center' | 'flex-end' | 'flex-start';
    justifyContent?: 'center' | 'flex-end' | 'flex-start' | 'space-around' | 'space-between' | 'space-evenly';
    display?: 'flex' | 'none';
    overflow?: 'hidden' | 'visible';
    width?: number | string;
    height?: number | string;
    minWidth?: number | string;
    minHeight?: number | string;
    gap?: number | 'xs' | 'sm' | 'md' | 'lg' | 'xl';
    columnGap?: ClaudeUiBoxLayout['gap'];
    rowGap?: ClaudeUiBoxLayout['gap'];
    margin?: ClaudeUiBoxLayout['gap'];
    marginX?: ClaudeUiBoxLayout['gap'];

         * Return the unified diff for the working copy vs HEAD. Pass a list of
         * paths to restrict to specific files; omit to get all changes. Used by
         * the Claude Code chat action to assemble a `diff` artifact after file-
         * mutating tool calls (Write/Edit/NotebookEdit).
         */
        getDiff(paths?: string[]): Promise<string>;
    };
    gh: {
        findPRForBranch(branch?: string): Promise<GhPullRequest | null>;
        getPRDetails(number: number, repo?: {
            owner: string;
            name: string;
        }): Promise<GhPullRequest & {
            comments: GhPRComment[];
        }>;
        getReviewThreads(number: number, repo?: {
            owner: string;
            name: string;
        }): Promise<GhReviewThread[]>;
    };
    /**
     * Claude Code wrapper. Highlights only — the full surface (sessions, mcp,
     * surfaces, skills, …) is available via `import { claudeCode } from
     * '#features/code/be/services/claude-code/index.ts'`.
     */
    /** Clear-cache resolve + exec test — same path as the Settings test button. */
    testCli(provider: string): Promise<{
        success: true;
        resolvedPath: string;
    } | {
        success: false;
        error: string;
    }>;
    claudeCode: {
        query(opts: Omit<QueryOptions, 'cwd'> & {
            cwd?: string;
        }): Promise<QueryHandle>;
        openConversation(key: string, launchKey: string, opts: Omit<QueryOptions, 'cwd' | 'prompt'> & {
            cwd?: string;
            accountedCostUsd?: number;
        }, consumeAutonomous: ClaudeTurnConsumer): Promise<ClaudeConversation>;
        getConversation(key: string): {
            owner: ClaudeConversation;
            launchKey: string;
        } | undefined;
        isConversationBusy(key: string): boolean;
        isCurrentTurn(key: string, handle: QueryHandle): boolean;
        closeIdleConversation(key: string): Promise<boolean>;
        /** Read a saved session's native plan without a prompt or transcript writes. */
        getPlan(opts: Omit<ClaudeSessionPlanOptions, 'cwd'> & {
            cwd?: string;
        }): Promise<ClaudePlan>;
        /** Read a saved session's native context summary without a prompt or transcript writes. */
        getContextUsage(opts: Omit<ClaudeSessionContextOptions, 'cwd'> & {
            cwd?: string;
        }): Promise<ClaudeContextUsage>;
        /** Validate native fork/resume membership without a prompt or persisted fork. */
        validateResumeTarget(opts: Omit<ClaudeSessionResumeTargetOptions, 'cwd'> & {
            cwd?: string;
        }): Promise<void>;
        getFileRewindPreview(opts: Omit<ClaudeSessionFileRewindOptions, 'cwd'> & {
            cwd?: string;
        }): Promise<ClaudeFileRewind>;
        rewindFiles(opts: Omit<ClaudeSessionFileRewindOptions, 'cwd'> & {
            cwd?: string;
        }): Promise<ClaudeFileRewind>;
        version(): Promise<string>;
        authStatus(): Promise<AuthStatus>;
        listSessions(opts?: SessionListOptions): Promise<SessionInfo[]>;
        /** List sessions across ALL project directories (not just the configured cwd). */
        listAllSessions(opts?: {
            limit?: number;
        }): Promise<SessionInfo[]>;
        /**
         * Parse a session's JSONL transcript into an in-memory array of entries.
         * Used by `CC: Handle Rewind` to retroactively backfill `context.cliUuid`
         * on pre-existing user messages from Claude's own session file.
         * `cwd` defaults to the configured project directory.
         */
        viewSession(id: string, opts?: Omit<SessionViewOptions, 'cwd'> & {
            cwd?: string;
        }): Promise<SessionTranscriptEntry[]>;
        /** Parse a JSONL file directly by path (bypasses cwd→bucket lookup). */
        viewSessionByFile(filePath: string, opts?: {
            limit?: number;
            offset?: number;
        }): Promise<SessionTranscriptEntry[]>;
        getWorkingDir(): string;
        /** Store a live query handle so other actions can write control_responses. */
        storeHandle(key: string, handle: QueryHandle): void;
        /** Retrieve a stored query handle by key (typically threadId). */
        getHandle(key: string): QueryHandle | undefined;
        /** Release an owned turn; idle process controls remain available until close/teardown. */
        clearHandle(key: string): void;
        /** Explicitly move an idle live conversation into its native terminal interface. */
        openNativeTerminal(key: string, options?: ClaudeNativeTerminalHandoffOptions): Promise<ClaudeNativeTerminalOwner>;
        getNativeTerminal(key: string): ClaudeNativeTerminalOwner | undefined;
        /**
         * Low-level one-shot CLI invocation. Used by `CC: Handle Revert` to run
         * `claude --resume <sid> --rewind-files <uuid>` for file-rewind on revert.
         * `cwd` defaults to the configured project directory.
         */
        exec(args: readonly string[], opts?: Omit<ExecOnceOptions, 'cwd'> & {
            cwd?: string;
        }): Promise<ExecOnceResult>;
        /** Read the CLI's user-scope settings.json (~/.claude/settings.json). */
        readSettings(): Promise<Record<string, any>>;
        /** Write the CLI's user-scope settings.json (~/.claude/settings.json). */
        writeSettings(settings: Record<string, any>): Promise<void>;
        /** Native model discovery can run session hooks when a temporary process is explicitly requested. */
        getModels(opts?: {
            cwd?: string;
            includeUnsupportedModels?: boolean;
        }): Promise<ClaudeModelCatalog>;
        /** List skill files from user (~/.claude/skills/) and project (.claude/skills/) dirs. */
        listSkills(): Promise<Array<{
            name: string;
            scope: string;
            path: string;
        }>>;
        /** List memory/CLAUDE.md files from known locations. */
        listMemoryFiles(): Promise<Array<{
            name: string;
            scope: string;
            path: string;
        }>>;
        /** Rename a Claude Code session through the native process. */
        renameSession(id: string, title: string, opts?: {
            cwd?: string;
        }): Promise<void>;
        /** Check whether a session JSONL file exists under the given (or default) project directory. */
        sessionExists(id: string, opts?: {
            cwd?: string;
        }): Promise<boolean>;
    };
    /** Codex CLI wrapper for one-shot tasks. */
    codex: {
        /**
         * Low-level one-shot CLI invocation via `codex exec`.
         * `cwd` defaults to the configured project directory.
         */
        exec(args: readonly string[], opts?: Omit<CodexExecOptions, 'cwd'> & {
            cwd?: string;
        }): Promise<CodexExecResult>;
    };
}

/**
 * What other features ask of the code surface: which panel to show, a write to its state, and a job for one of its
 * children. The `<child>.*` events aren't in this machine's own union — it routes them to its child actors by
 * prefix — so they are spelled out, and each names the child that handles it.
 */
type CodeInboxEvent = {
    type: 'UPDATE_STATE';
    updates: Partial<CodeContext>;
} | {
    type: 'terminal.CREATE';
    target: string;
    command: string;
    cwd?: string;
} | {
    type: 'explorer.SET_BASE_DIRECTORY';
    path: string;
} | {
    type: 'codeActions.OPEN_ACTION';
    actionId: EARS.EntityId;
} | {
    type: 'codePrompts.OPEN_PROMPT';
    promptId: EARS.EntityId;
};

type CodeSettings = SettingsOf<'code'>;

interface CodeSystemContext {
    baseDirectory: string | null;
    /**
     * The default directory the settings named when this system last heard them. The settings arrive whenever any of
     * the code settings change, the browsed `baseDirectory` included, so only a new default moves the explorer.
     */
    defaultBaseDirectory: string | null;
    gitRepository: GitRepository | null;
    gitWatcher: GitWatcherService | null;

interface CodeSystemError {
    code: 'FILE_TOO_LARGE' | 'INVALID_PATH' | 'IO_ERROR' | 'NOT_FOUND' | 'PERMISSION_DENIED' | 'SEARCH_ERROR';

    path?: string;
}

 * Low-level one-shot process primitive for the Codex CLI.
 *
 * Mirrors `claude-code/runner.ts:execOnce` but resolves the `codex` binary
 * and uses Codex-appropriate env scrubbing.
 */

    cwd?: string;

interface CodexExecResult {

interface DatabaseContext {

    pendingTransactionCall: string | null;

        type: 'attribute' | 'entity' | 'relation';
        value: string;

    mode: 'query' | 'transaction';
    isAiQueryLoading: boolean;
    isRefreshing: boolean;
    settings: DatabaseSettings | null;
    viewMode: 'database' | 'runs';
    flowRuns: RunNode[];
    currentFlowId: string | null;
    flowEvents: RunTree[];
    expandedNodes: Set<string>;
    nodeDetails: Map<string, RunNode>;
    isLoadingRuns: boolean;
    runPagination: {
        offset: number;
        limit: number;
        hasMore: boolean;
    };
    backupInfo: {
        timestamp: number;
        databases: string[];
        size: number;
        hasMedia?: boolean;
    } | null;
    exporting: boolean;
    importing: boolean;
    /** The last export or import to finish, a new object each time */

}

/**
 * Low-level process primitives for the Claude Code wrapper.
 *
 * Two flavours, because the CLI has two very different execution modes:
 *
 * 1. `execOnce` — one-shot subcommands (`claude mcp list`, `claude auth status`,
 *    …). Resolves when the child exits. Uses a bounded timeout. Translates
 *    spawn/exit errors into typed `ClaudeCodeError` subclasses.
 *
 * 2. `spawnStream` — long-lived `claude -p --input-format stream-json
 *    --output-format stream-json` for interactive conversations. Returns a
 *    handle the caller uses to push user turns (stdin) and iterate events
 *    (stdout) until the child exits. Abort via `AbortSignal`.
 *
 * Neither primitive knows anything about the wire protocol — they just move
 * bytes. The stream-json schema lives in `types.ts` and parsing in `ndjson.ts`.
 */

interface ExecOnceOptions {
    cwd?: string;
    /**
     * Merge overrides into the inherited child environment. ANTHROPIC_API_KEY
     * and NODE_ENV are always removed, including explicit overrides. Native
     * file checkpointing defaults on only when its environment switch is unset.
     */
    env?: NodeJS.ProcessEnv;
    /** Bytes piped to stdin. Pass `undefined` to leave stdin closed. */
    input?: string;
    /** Milliseconds until the child is SIGKILL'd and a ClaudeTimeoutError thrown. */
    timeoutMs?: number;
    signal?: AbortSignal;
    /** Override the resolved CLI path (primarily for testing). */
    cliPath?: string;
}

interface ExecOnceResult {
    stdout: string;
    stderr: string;
    exitCode: number;
}

     */
    getFileContentFromBranchAsDataUrl(filePath: string, branch: string): Promise<string>;
    /** Check if a file path is an image based on extension. */
    isImageFile(filePath: string): boolean;
    /** Execute a git command and return raw binary buffer output. */
    private executeGitCommandBinary;
    stageFiles(filePaths: string[]): Promise<void>;
    unstageFiles(filePaths: string[]): Promise<void>;
    revertFile(filePath: string): Promise<void>;
    revertFiles(filePaths: string[]): Promise<void>;
    fileExistsInHead(filePath: string): Promise<boolean>;
    resolveConflict(filePath: string, strategy: 'ours' | 'theirs'): Promise<void>;
    commit(message: string): Promise<void>;
    isGitRepository(): Promise<boolean>;
    hasUncommittedChanges(): Promise<boolean>;
    getStagedFiles(): Promise<GitStatusFile[]>;
    getUpstreamBranch(): Promise<string | null>;
    getBaseBranch(options?: {
        preferUpstream?: boolean;
    }): Promise<string>;
    getPRBaseBranch(): Promise<string>;
    /**
     * Find the closest parent branch by comparing merge-base distances.
     * When branching feature-B from feature-A, feature-A will have a much
     * smaller commit distance than main/master.
     */
    private findClosestParentBranch;
    private getCommitDistance;
    private getNearbyMergedBranches;
    getBranchDiff(baseBranch: string, targetBranch?: string): Promise<GitStatusFile[]>;
    getFileDiffBetweenBranches(filePath: string, baseBranch: string, targetBranch?: string): Promise<string>;
    getFileContentFromBranch(filePath: string, branch: string): Promise<string>;
    fetchRemoteBranch(branch: string): Promise<void>;
    deleteRemoteBranch(branch: string): Promise<void>;
    getAllBranches(): Promise<string[]>;
    checkoutBranch(branchName: string): Promise<void>;
    isCurrentBranchPublished(): Promise<boolean>;
    private _forceFetchNext;
    /**
     * Force a fetch on the next getCommitsAheadBehind() call,
     * regardless of the auto-fetch toggle or throttle timer.
     */
    forceFetchOnce(): void;
    private fetchIfStale;
    getCommitsAheadBehind(): Promise<{
        ahead: number;
        behind: number;
    }>;
    pushBranch(): Promise<void>;
    pullBranch(): Promise<void>;
    stashPush(message?: string, stagedOnly?: boolean): Promise<string>;
    stashList(): Promise<StashEntry[]>;
    stashApply(index: number): Promise<void>;
    stashPop(index: number): Promise<void>;
    private isStashConflict;
    stashDrop(index: number): Promise<void>;
    stashClear(): Promise<void>;
    worktreeList(): Promise<WorktreeEntry[]>;
    worktreeAdd(wtPath: string, branch?: string, createBranch?: boolean): Promise<string>;
    worktreeRemove(wtPath: string, force?: boolean): Promise<void>;
    getCommitsBetweenBranches(baseBranch: string, targetBranch?: string): Promise<{
        subject: string;
        body: string;
    }[]>;
    gitLog(count?: number): Promise<CommitLogEntry[]>;
    revertCommit(hash: string): Promise<void>;
    resetToCommit(hash: string): Promise<void>;
}

interface GitStatusFile {
    path: string;
    status: 'added' | 'copied' | 'deleted' | 'modified' | 'renamed' | 'typechange' | 'unmerged' | 'untracked';
    staged: boolean;
    originalPath?: string;
    score?: number;
}

declare class GitWatcherService {
    private workingDirectory;
    private gitWatcher?;
    private workingDirWatcher?;
    private onGitChangeCallback?;
    private onFileChangeCallback?;
    private gitStatusDebounceTimeout?;
    private fileChangeTimeouts;
    private isWatching;
    private openFiles;
    private gitRepository?;
    constructor(workingDirectory: string);
    setGitRepository(repo: GitRepository): void;
    setChangeCallback(callback: () => void): void;
    setFileChangeCallback(callback: (change: FileChangeInfo) => void): void;
    registerOpenFile(filePath: string): void;
    unregisterOpenFile(filePath: string): void;
    isFileOpen(filePath: string): boolean;
    getOpenFiles(): string[];
    startWatching(signal: AbortSignal): Promise<void>;
    private handleFileChange;
    stopWatching(): Promise<void>;
    isActive(): boolean;
}

/** A model services.inference runs, named by its provider:model id */
interface InferenceEmbeddingModel extends EmbeddingModelInfo {

}

type KeepAliveLine = z.infer<typeof KeepAliveLineSchema>;

/** `keep_alive` — NDJSON heartbeat, silently ignored by readers. */
declare const KeepAliveLineSchema: z.ZodObject<{
    type: z.ZodLiteral<"keep_alive">;
}, "strip", z.ZodTypeAny, {
    type: "keep_alive";
}, {
    type: "keep_alive";
}>;

interface KeepAliveNode extends NodeBase {

/**
 * `fetchedAt` is when these details were read from GitHub, and the viewer keeps the newest per PR so a
 * slow retry cannot overwrite fresher data from a concurrent fetch.
 *
 * **A clock rather than a counter, because the two sides do not share a lifetime.** What this is
 * compared against lives in the renderer, which outlives the backend: anything reset by an API restart
 * or a pack reload reads as older there forever after, and every later answer is dropped.
 */
 | {
    type: 'pr.PR_DETAILS_RECEIVED';
    data: {

type OutgoingTerminalEvents = 
/**
 * A terminal exists — news for every window, so every list grows. It carries no instruction: a window that
 * did not ask for it must not open it, which is what `terminal.OPENED` is for.
 */
{
    type: 'terminal.CREATED';
    data: TerminalInfo;
}
/**
 * The terminal *you* asked for, answered to the window that asked.
 *
 * Its id does not exist until it is created, so nothing the asker sent could identify it — which is why
 * this is addressed rather than keyed. Before it existed, `terminal.CREATED` carried both jobs and every
 * window routed a new terminal into its own panel.
 */
 | {
    type: 'terminal.OPENED';
    data: TerminalInfo;
} | {
    type: 'terminal.OUTPUT';
    data: {
        terminalId: string;
        data: string;
    };
} | {
    type: 'terminal.INITIAL_OUTPUT';
    data: {
        terminalId: string;
        data: string;
    };
} | {
    type: 'terminal.CLOSED';
    data: {
        terminalId: string;
    };
} | {
    type: 'terminal.RENAMED';
    data: {
        terminalId: string;
        customTitle: string;
    };
} | {
    type: 'terminal.CWD_CHANGED';
    data: {
        terminalId: string;
        cwd: string;
        title?: string;
    };
} | {
    type: 'terminal.ERROR';
    data: {
        message: string;
        terminalId?: string;
    };
} | {
    type: 'terminal.TERMINALS_LISTED';
    data: TerminalInfo[];
} | {
    type: 'terminal.TERMINAL_TAB_OPENED';
    data: TerminalInfo;
};

/** Entity shapes this pack declares */
type OwnEntityShapes = {
    'Thread': ThreadShape & {
        shortCode: ShortCode<'THR'>;
    };
    'Message': MessageEntity;
    'Artifact': ArtifactEntity;
    'Terminal': TerminalEntity;
    'Note': NoteEntity;
    'BrowserTab': BrowserTabEntity;
    'BrowserBookmark': BrowserBookmarkEntity;
};

/** The repositories this pack declares (apack.json features[].repositories) */
type OwnRepositories = {
    threadQueries: typeof threadQueries;
    threadCommands: typeof threadCommands;
    chatQueries: typeof chatQueries;
    chatCommands: typeof chatCommands;
    terminalQueries: typeof terminalQueries;
    terminalCommands: typeof terminalCommands;
    noteQueries: typeof noteQueries;
    noteCommands: typeof noteCommands;
    browserQueries: typeof browserQueries;
    browserCommands: typeof browserCommands;
    libraryQueries: typeof libraryQueries;
    libraryCommands: typeof libraryCommands;
    flowsQueries: typeof flowsQueries;
    flowsCommands: typeof flowsCommands;
    actionQueries: typeof actionQueries;
    actionCommands: typeof actionCommands;
    promptQueries: typeof promptQueries;
    promptCommands: typeof promptCommands;
    brainQueries: typeof brainQueries;
    brainCommands: typeof brainCommands;
};

/**
 * Surface id → the events that surface receives: its own feature's system's, and every audience of the inbox its
 * contract declares. This pack's own sends are checked against it, so a sibling may send the `pack` half.
 */
type OwnSurfaceEvents = {
    'threads': __events_threads | __accepts_threads;
    'code': __events_code | __accepts_code;
    'notes': __events_notes | __accepts_notes;
    'browser': __events_browser | __accepts_browser;
    'library': __events_library | __accepts_library;
    'flows': __events_flows | __accepts_flows;
    'actions': __events_actions | __accepts_actions;
    'prompts': __events_prompts | __accepts_prompts;
    'brain': __events_brain | __accepts_brain;
    'database': __events_database | __accepts_database;
    'logs': __events_logs | __accepts_logs;
};

/**
 * `services.emitter`, typed with this pack's events. A system and a surface are both named
 * `<pack>/<feature>`, this pack's own and the host's too; a system may also be a role.
 *
 * Naming its own pack is the point, not a gap left by the action having no pack scope. An action is content,
 * not source: a row a user can edit in the Actions surface, read in the DB console, export to a content file and
 * copy into another pack. A bare name would rebind on that copy — `'threads'` quietly meaning the new pack's
 * feature, or nothing — where a ref that no longer fits is wrong visibly, and is refused at the bus rather
 * than doing something else.
 */
type PackEmitter = Omit<HostServices['emitter'], 'broadcastToSurface' | 'sendToSystem' | 'sendToWindow'> & {
    broadcastToSurface: TypedSendToSurface<QualifiedSurfaceEvents>;
    sendToWindow: TypedSendToWindow<QualifiedSurfaceEvents>;
    sendToSystem: TypedSendToSystem<QualifiedSystemEvents>;
};

type PackFeatureSettings = {
    'threads': __FeatureSettings_Threads.Value;
    'code': __FeatureSettings_Code.Value;
    'notes': __FeatureSettings_Notes.Value;
    'browser': __FeatureSettings_Browser.Value;
    'library': __FeatureSettings_Library.Value;
    'flows': __FeatureSettings_Flows.Value;
    'actions': __FeatureSettings_Actions.Value;
    'prompts': __FeatureSettings_Prompts.Value;
    'brain': __FeatureSettings_Brain.Value;
    'database': __FeatureSettings_Database.Value;
    'logs': __FeatureSettings_Logs.Value;
};

/** Local pack preference groups, inferred from schemas rather than default literals. */
type PackGroupSettings = {
    'assistant': __PackSettings_Assistant.Value;
};

/**
 * Every entity shape this pack can read: the SDK's, its own and its dependencies'. Node is the union of
 * the step node types (NodeBase when no step defines one), not an intersection of each pack's.
 */
type PackShapes = Omit<SdkEntityShapes & OwnEntityShapes, 'Node'> & {
    Node: [PackStepNodes] extends [never] ? SdkEntityShapes['Node'] : PackStepNodes;
};

/** The step node types of this pack and its dependencies; never when none defines one */
type PackStepNodes = NodeEntity;

/**
 * Feature id → the `public` half of the inbox this pack's surface declares (dependents name it
 * `default-setup/<feature>`). Neither its own system's events nor its `pack` audience are here: the first is
 * between the two halves of one feature, the second between this pack's features. What is left is what a dependent
 * pack may send, and it mirrors `PackSystemEvents`.
 */
type PackSurfaceEvents = {
    'threads': __public_threads;
    'code': __public_code;
    'notes': __public_notes;
    'browser': __public_browser;
    'library': __public_library;
    'flows': __public_flows;
    'actions': __public_actions;
    'prompts': __public_prompts;
    'brain': __public_brain;
    'database': __public_database;
    'logs': __public_logs;
};

/** Feature id → the state this pack's surface for that feature publishes (dependents name it `default-setup/<feature>`). */
type PackSurfaceState = {
    'threads': SurfaceStateOf<Contract$a>;
    'code': SurfaceStateOf<Contract$9>;
    'notes': SurfaceStateOf<Contract$8>;
    'browser': SurfaceStateOf<Contract$7>;
    'library': SurfaceStateOf<Contract$6>;
    'flows': SurfaceStateOf<Contract$5>;
    'actions': SurfaceStateOf<Contract$4>;
    'prompts': SurfaceStateOf<Contract$3>;
    'brain': SurfaceStateOf<Contract$2>;
    'database': SurfaceStateOf<Contract$1>;
    'logs': SurfaceStateOf<Contract>;
};

/** Feature id → the events this pack's system for that feature receives (dependents name it `default-setup/<feature>`). */
type PackSystemEvents = {
    'threads': IncomingEventsOf<SystemContracts['threads']>;
    'code': IncomingEventsOf<SystemContracts['code']>;
    'notes': IncomingEventsOf<SystemContracts['notes']>;
    'browser': IncomingEventsOf<SystemContracts['browser']>;
    'library': IncomingEventsOf<SystemContracts['library']>;
    'flows': IncomingEventsOf<SystemContracts['flows']>;
    'actions': IncomingEventsOf<SystemContracts['actions']>;
    'prompts': IncomingEventsOf<SystemContracts['prompts']>;
    'brain': IncomingEventsOf<SystemContracts['brain']>;
    'database': IncomingEventsOf<SystemContracts['database']>;
    'logs': IncomingEventsOf<SystemContracts['logs']>;
};

type PanelType = 'actions' | 'commit' | 'explorer' | 'pr' | 'prompts' | 'search';

/**
 * Every line type we explicitly recognise. Each variant has a literal
 * `type` discriminator so a `switch(line.type)` narrows exhaustively
 * without casts. Used internally by `pump()` in `query.ts`.
 */
/** Emitted by the pump when a JSON line fails to parse. */
interface ParseErrorLine {
    type: '__parse_error';
    raw: string;
    error: string;
}

/**
 * The response shape for `can_use_tool`, as required by the Claude Code
 * CLI at:
 *   packages/claude-code/src/utils/permissions/PermissionPromptToolResultSchema.ts
 *
 * - `allow` MUST include `updatedInput` (Record<string, unknown>). The CLI
 *   treats an empty object as "run with the original tool input", so
 *   callers that don't intend to modify the input should echo `req.input`
 *   back verbatim — this is the safer default.
 * - `deny` MUST include a `message` string. Callers that don't have a
 *   specific reason should send a generic "User denied".
 *
 * Malformed responses (missing required fields) are rejected by the CLI
 * with a `ZodError: invalid_union` that surfaces as "Tool permission
 * request failed: …" on the user's tool-activity row. Both required
 * fields are enforced statically here so that class of bug can't
 * silently reoccur at the call site.
 */
type PermissionDecision = {
    behavior: 'allow';
    updatedInput: Record<string, unknown>;
    updatedPermissions?: Array<Record<string, unknown>>;
    toolUseID?: string;
    decisionClassification?: 'user_permanent' | 'user_reject' | 'user_temporary';
} | {
    behavior: 'deny';
    message: string;
    interrupt?: boolean;
    toolUseID?: string;
    decisionClassification?: 'user_permanent' | 'user_reject' | 'user_temporary';
};

/** Caller hook: decide a tool permission request. */
type PermissionHandler = (request: CanUseToolRequest) => PermissionDecision | Promise<PermissionDecision>;

type PermissionMode = z.infer<typeof PermissionModeSchema>;

/**
 * Permission modes accepted by `claude --permission-mode`. Names match the
 * CLI's Commander validator exactly (see the leaked source at
 * `src/types/permissions.ts` or the error message the CLI prints when you
 * pass an unknown value).
 *
 * Interoperation note: only `default`, `plan`, and `acceptEdits` emit
 * `can_use_tool` control_requests that our wrapper's `onPermissionRequest`
 * hook can intercept. `bypassPermissions` and `dontAsk` short-circuit the
 * permission resolver entirely; `auto` is feature-gated and uses an ML
 * classifier instead of prompting.
 */
declare const PermissionModeSchema: z.ZodEnum<["default", "acceptEdits", "plan", "bypassPermissions", "dontAsk", "auto"]>;

type Predicate = {
    key: string;
    operator: BinaryOperator;
    value?: any;
} | ((context: any) => boolean);

declare class PromptService {
    findByLabel(label: string): _apackdev_sdk.PromptEntity | undefined;
    /**
     * Execute a template with prompt context for accessing other prompts
     * @param templateFn - The template function body
     * @param templateParams - Parameters to pass to the template
     */
    private executeTemplate;
    /**
     * Get and execute a prompt by label
     * @param label - The prompt label
     * @param templateParams - Parameters to pass to the template
     */
    usePrompt(label: string, templateParams: Record<string, any>): string | undefined;
}

interface PromptTab {
    path: string;
    content: string;
    modified: boolean;
    isPrompt: true;
    promptEntity: PromptEntity;
    isDiff?: boolean;
    externallyModified?: boolean;
    externalModificationTime?: Date;
    pendingSaveConflict?: boolean;

     * **A domain id cannot do this job, for two independent reasons.** It does not distinguish windows, so
     * another window's answer for the same prompt satisfies it; and it does not distinguish asks, so a slot

     * surface. A call is per ask and per window, and the slot is cleared when its answer lands.

        status: 'error' | 'exporting' | 'idle' | 'success';
        errors: string[];

        promptCount: number;
    };

        label: string;
        description?: string;
        category?: string;
        inputs: Record<string, TemplateInput>;
        templateFn: string;

declare const brainCommands: {
    /** An event a flow run heard, with its payload as the node's attributes */
    readonly createEventRunNode: (eventNode: {
        id?: string;
        label?: string;
        eventType?: string;
        triggerType?: string;
        cronExpression?: string;
    }, flowRunNodeId: RunNodeId, payload: unknown) => RunNode;
    readonly createFlowRunNode: (flowStepId: EARS.EntityId, parentId?: RunNodeId, executionContext?: ExecutionContext) => {
        flowRunNode: RunNode;
        flowId: EARS.EntityId;
        input: StepInput;
    };
    readonly createStepRunNode: (stepId: EARS.EntityId, parentId: RunNodeId, executionContext?: ExecutionContext) => {
        runNode: RunNode;
        step: NodeEntity;
        input: StepInput;
    };
    /** A new session's root run: the root flow the brain starts with, under a new id each time it starts */
    readonly createRootFlowRunNode: () => {
        rootFlow: Pick<FlowEntity, "createdAt" | "flowType" | "id" | "label">;
        rootFlowRunNode: RunNode;
    };
};

declare const brainQueries: {
    readonly runNodeById: (id: RunNodeId) => RunNode | undefined;
    readonly eventAllSteps: (eventNodeId: EARS.EntityId) => NodeEntity[];
    readonly nextNodeInFlowTrack: (nodeId: EARS.EntityId) => NodeEntity;
    readonly nextNodeForBranch: (nodeId: EARS.EntityId, sourceHandle?: string) => NodeEntity | undefined;
    /** The event tracks a flow node heard, each with the nodes it ran under it: a flow node's children are its events */
    readonly eventTracks: (flowRunNodeId: RunNodeId) => RunTree[];
    readonly possibleEvents: (flowRunNodeId: RunNodeId) => EventListenerEntity[];
    /** The flow node a node ran under: the nearest flow above it, or undefined for a session's root */
    readonly parentFlowOf: typeof parentFlowOf;
    /**
     * Builds flow hierarchy from current flow back to root
     * Returns array ordered from root → current flow
     */
    readonly buildFlowHierarchy: (flowRunNodeId: RunNodeId) => Array<{
        flowRunNodeId: EARS.EntityId;
        label: string;
    }>;
    /**
     * Where a run node's blueprint is, for revealing it in the Flows surface: the node it ran and the flow holding
     * it. A subflow's node sits in the flow its parent runs, not in the flow it runs itself.
     */
    readonly revealIn: (id: RunNodeId) => {
        flowId: EARS.EntityId;
        nodeId: EARS.EntityId;
    } | undefined;
    readonly extendedRunNodeData: (runNodeId: RunNodeId) => FlowRunNodeData;
    /** The session's root run, or nothing to show when the brain runs none */
    readonly rootData: (rootRunId: RunNodeId | undefined) => FlowRunNodeData;
};

declare const browserCommands: {
    readonly syncTabs: (tabs: SavedTab[]) => void;
    readonly syncBookmarks: (bookmarks: SavedBookmark[]) => void;
};

declare const browserQueries: {
    readonly allTabs: () => SavedTab[];
    readonly allBookmarks: () => SavedBookmark[];
};

/**
 * Database Service
 *
 * `services.database`: live-data context for AI query generation. Actions read and write
 * entities through `services.repository`; pack code imports `query`/`create`/`write` from `#generated/ears`.
 */
/**
 * Build a query context from live data for AI query generation.
 * Samples one entity per type to extract real attribute names + values,
 * and maps the relationship topology.
 */
declare function buildQueryContext(): {
    schema: string;
    topology: string;
};

declare const chatCommands: {
    readonly addMessage: (params: {
        threadId: EARS.EntityId;
        text: string;
        sender: "assistant" | "marker" | "system" | "user";
        blocks?: BlockConfig[];
        forkable?: boolean;
        references?: MessageReferences;
        isCommand?: boolean;
        command?: string;
        autoHide?: boolean;
        asUser?: boolean;
        asideContext?: string;
        blockResponse?: any;
        responseTimestamp?: number;
        asideText?: string;
        compacted?: boolean;
        context?: Record<string, unknown>;
        skipRelink?: boolean;
    }) => {
        id: EARS.EntityId;
        threadId: EARS.EntityId;
        text: string;
        sender: string;
        timestamp: number;
    };
    readonly createThreadFromMessage: (text: string) => {
        threadId: EARS.EntityId;
        threadData: ReturnType<typeof threadCommands.create>;
    };
    readonly updateMessageBlockResponse: (params: {
        messageId: EARS.EntityId;
        response: any;
    }) => {
        messageId: EARS.EntityId;
        responseTimestamp: number;
        updatedAt: number;
        blocks?: BlockConfig[];
    };
    readonly updateMessageState: (params: {
        messageId: EARS.EntityId;
        updates: Partial<Pick<MessageEntity, "blockResponse" | "blocks" | "compacted" | "context" | "forkable" | "references" | "responseTimestamp" | "status" | "text">>;
    }) => {
        messageId: EARS.EntityId;
        updatedAt: number;
        updates: typeof params.updates;
    };
    readonly createMarkerMessage: (params: {
        threadId: EARS.EntityId;
        text: string;
    }) => {
        id: EARS.EntityId;
        threadId: EARS.EntityId;
        text: string;
        sender: string;
        timestamp: number;
        compactedMessageIds: EARS.EntityId[];
    };
    readonly toggleMarkerCompacted: (markerId: EARS.EntityId, compacted: boolean) => EARS.EntityId[];
    readonly copyMessagesUpTo: (params: {
        sourceThreadId: EARS.EntityId;
        targetThreadId: EARS.EntityId;
        upToMessageId: string;
    }) => void;
    readonly softDeleteMessagesAfter: (params: {
        threadId: EARS.EntityId;
        messageId: EARS.EntityId;
    }) => {
        deletedCount: number;
        deletedIds: string[];
    };
    readonly createArtifact: (params: {
        artifactType: ArtifactType;
        title: string;
        content: any;
        threadId?: EARS.EntityId;
        color?: string;
    }) => {
        artifactId: EARS.EntityId;
    };
    /**
     * Patch an existing artifact's title and/or content in place.
     *
     * Used by `services.artifact.updateAndNotify` for artifacts that need to
     * mutate across turns (e.g. the Claude Code session card, which tracks
     * live status / cost / turn count).
     */
    readonly updateArtifact: (artifactId: EARS.EntityId, patch: {
        title?: string;
        content?: unknown;
    }) => void;
    /**
     * Find an artifact by (thread, artifactType). Used by upsert helpers to
     * avoid creating duplicates on repeated turns. Returns the first match
     * (there should only ever be one for singleton types like claude-session).
     */
    readonly findArtifactByType: (threadId: EARS.EntityId, artifactType: ArtifactType) => ArtifactEntity | undefined;
};

    readonly threadArtifacts: (threadId: EARS.EntityId) => ArtifactItem<unknown>[];

    readonly paginatedMessages: typeof paginatedMessages;
    readonly refreshThreadsData: () => RecentThreadRefreshData;

    readonly hasMessageInThread: (messageId: EARS.EntityId, threadId: EARS.EntityId) => boolean;
    readonly messageById: (messageId: EARS.EntityId) => MessageEntity | null;
};

/**
 * Create a new artifact and notify the frontend (with side effects)
 *
 * This function creates an artifact and automatically sends ARTIFACT_ADDED event
 * to the frontend when a threadId is provided. Use this in flow actions where
 * you want immediate UI updates.
 *
 * @param options - Options for creating the artifact
 * @returns Object containing the created artifact ID
 *
 * @example
 * // Create artifact with automatic FE notification
 * const { artifactId } = createAndNotify({
 *   artifactType: 'todo',
 *   title: 'Tasks',
 *   content: { tasks: [...] },
 *   threadId: 'thread-123'
 * });
 * // Frontend automatically receives ARTIFACT_ADDED event
 */
declare function createAndNotify(options: CreateArtifactOptions): {
    artifactId: EARS.EntityId;
};

/** Process-local reverse UI requests. These never enter a conversation turn or permission queue. */
declare function createClaudePromptHosts(write: (value: unknown) => boolean): {
    register(client: string, host: ClaudePromptHost): () => void;
    answers(client: string): string[];
    detach: (client: string) => void;
    receive(line: ControlRequestLine): boolean;
    cancel(id: string): boolean;
    close(): void;
};

/** Native UI transport only. A renderer must validate tree nodes before displaying them. */
declare function createClaudeUiControls(initialized: Promise<Pick<ClaudeInitialization, 'capabilities'>>, controls: Pick<ReturnType<typeof createHostControls>, 'request'>, promptHosts?: Pick<ReturnType<typeof createClaudePromptHosts>, 'answers' | 'detach' | 'register'>): {
    clientEvent(options: {
        client_id: string;
        id: string;
        sequence: number;
        event: ClaudeUiClientInput;
    }): Promise<{
        handled: boolean;
    }>;
    /** Register only a mounted composer; registration alone does not attach or create a process. */
    servePrompt(id: string, host: ClaudePromptHost): () => void;
    /** Advertise only reverse APIs the attached view can actually answer. */
    attach(options: ClaudeUiAttach): Promise<z.objectOutputType<{
        surfaces: z.ZodArray<z.ZodEnum<["terminal", "desktop", "mobile", "vscode"]>, "many">;
    }, z.ZodTypeAny, "passthrough">>;
    detach(id: string): Promise<z.objectOutputType<{
        detached: z.ZodBoolean;
        surfaces: z.ZodArray<z.ZodEnum<["terminal", "desktop", "mobile", "vscode"]>, "many">;
    }, z.ZodTypeAny, "passthrough">>;
    render(options: ClaudeUiRender): Promise<ClaudeUiRenderResult>;
    /** Read on mount: a pane opened before stream arming produces no roster push. */
    panes(id: string): Promise<ClaudeUiPaneRoster>;
    showPane(options: z.infer<typeof paneSelection>): Promise<z.objectOutputType<{
        shown_id: z.ZodNullable<z.ZodString>;
    }, z.ZodTypeAny, "passthrough">>;
    /** Null returns the keyboard and settles a refused plugin focus request. */
    focusPane(options: Omit<z.infer<typeof paneSelection>, "id"> & {
        id: string | null;
    }): Promise<z.objectOutputType<{
        focused_id: z.ZodNullable<z.ZodString>;
    }, z.ZodTypeAny, "passthrough">>;
    /** Native runs ui.close with origin person; a hook may refuse, so never hide optimistically. */
    closePane(options: {
        client_id: string;
        id: string;
    }): Promise<z.objectOutputType<{
        closed: z.ZodBoolean;
    }, z.ZodTypeAny, "passthrough">>;
    /** Native owns scroll hooks and the authoritative offset, including a denied move. */
    scroll(options: ClaudeUiScroll): Promise<z.objectOutputType<{
        moved: z.ZodBoolean;
        offset: z.ZodNumber;
        deny: z.ZodOptional<z.ZodString>;
        follow_end: z.ZodOptional<z.ZodBoolean>;
    }, z.ZodTypeAny, "passthrough">>;
    /** Hooks may redirect the ring; the mounted owner validates the returned identity before applying it. */
    focus(options: ClaudeUiFocus): Promise<ClaudeUiFocusResult>;
    /** Native owns prompt.edit hooks and latest-edit coalescing; app adoption raises no edit hook. */
    promptEdit(options: ClaudeUiPromptEdit): Promise<ClaudeUiPromptFrame>;
    /** Plugin rows follow the host's own completion rows; autocomplete never gates an edit or Send. */
    promptAutocomplete(options: ClaudeUiPromptAutocomplete): Promise<ClaudeUiPromptCompletions>;
    /** Native serializes callbacks per plugin; send the whole current draft on each edit. */
    input(options: ClaudeUiInput): Promise<z.objectOutputType<{
        handled: z.ZodBoolean;
        element: z.ZodOptional<z.ZodString>;
        value: z.ZodOptional<z.ZodString>;
    }, z.ZodTypeAny, "passthrough">>;
    /** Native validates membership against the Select options in the current drawing. */
    select(options: ClaudeUiSelect): Promise<z.objectOutputType<{
        handled: z.ZodBoolean;
        element: z.ZodOptional<z.ZodString>;
        value: z.ZodOptional<z.ZodString>;
    }, z.ZodTypeAny, "passthrough">>;
    /** Forward the capability issued in the current tree; a stale handle returns handled:false. */
    press(options: ClaudeUiPress): Promise<z.objectOutputType<{
        handled: z.ZodBoolean;
        element: z.ZodOptional<z.ZodString>;
    }, z.ZodTypeAny, "passthrough">>;
};

/** Host-to-CLI requests are independent of CLI-to-host tool permission requests. */
declare function createHostControls(write: (value: unknown) => boolean): {
    request(request: Record<string, unknown> & {
        subtype: string;
    }, timeoutMs?: number): Promise<unknown>;
    receive(value: unknown): void;
    close(error?: Error): void;
};

 * Create a marker message that compacts eligible prior messages in a thread.
 * The repository determines which messages are eligible (excludes markers and already-compacted).
 */

    threadId: EARS.EntityId;
    text: string;

 *
 * @param options - Thread creation options

 *   role: EARS.RoleKind.ASSISTANT_BIRTH,
 *   forcedMode: 'birth'
 * });
 */
declare function createThreadAndNotify(options: ThreadCreateData): {
    id: EARS.EntityId;
    shortCode: string;
    timestamp: number;
    status: string;
};

declare const libraryCommands: {
    /** Follows every write above, until the returned function is called */
    readonly onDocumentsChanged: (listener: DocumentsListener) => () => void;
    readonly createDocument: (name: string, content: ContentSection[], tags: string[], collectionId?: EARS.EntityId, id?: string, contentHash?: string, documentType?: string) => DocumentDTO;
    readonly updateDocument: (id: EARS.EntityId, name: string, content: ContentSection[], tags: string[], collectionId?: EARS.EntityId, contentHash?: string, documentType?: string) => DocumentDTO;
    readonly deleteDocument: (id: EARS.EntityId) => void;
    readonly createCollection: (name: string, description?: string, parentId?: EARS.EntityId, id?: string, contentHash?: string) => CollectionDTO;
    readonly updateCollection: (id: EARS.EntityId, name: string, description?: string, contentHash?: string) => CollectionDTO;
    readonly deleteCollection: (id: EARS.EntityId) => void;
    /** A document with no folder given moves to the root, which is `null` to the repository */
    readonly moveDocument: (documentId: EARS.EntityId, newCollectionId?: EARS.EntityId) => DocumentDTO;
    readonly renameItem: (id: EARS.EntityId, name: string, type: "document" | "folder") => LibraryItem;
    readonly deleteItems: (ids: EARS.EntityId[]) => void;
    readonly moveItems: (ids: EARS.EntityId[], targetFolderId: EARS.EntityId | null) => void;
    readonly createSymlinkCollection: (name: string, symlinkPath: string, parentId?: EARS.EntityId, id?: string) => CollectionDTO;
    readonly updateSymlinkPath: (collectionId: EARS.EntityId, newPath: string) => CollectionDTO;
    readonly updateDocumentTags: (documentId: EARS.EntityId, tags: string[]) => void;
};

declare const libraryQueries: {
    readonly getDocuments: (collectionId?: string) => DocumentDTO[];
    readonly findDocument: (id: EARS.EntityId) => DocumentDTO | null;

    readonly findDocumentByShortCode: (shortCode: DocumentShortCode) => DocumentDTO | null;
    /** Every document and folder by name, for the reference picker and the panel's stats */
    readonly getIndex: () => LibraryIndex;
    readonly getCollections: () => CollectionDTO[];
    readonly getFolderContents: (folderId: EARS.EntityId | null) => Promise<FolderContents>;
    readonly getFolderPath: (folderId: EARS.EntityId | null) => BreadcrumbItem[];
    readonly getParentFolderId: (folderId: EARS.EntityId) => EARS.EntityId | null;
    readonly getDocumentsInCollection: (collectionId: EARS.EntityId) => DocumentDTO[];
    readonly getText: (id: EARS.EntityId) => string | undefined;

    limit?: number;
}): Promise<CodexSessionInfo[]>;

 * Returns an unsubscribe function for cleanup.
 *
 * If a named `id` is provided and already exists, the old listener is replaced.
 */
declare function listen(eventType: string, callback: BrainEventCallback, options?: ListenOptions): () => void;

declare const noteCommands: {
    readonly create: (input: {
        title: string;
        content?: string;
        icon?: string | null;
        parentId?: string;
        displayOrder?: number;
        noteType?: "document" | "task" | "tasklist";
        completed?: boolean;
        id?: string;
    }) => NoteEntity;
    readonly update: (id: EARS.EntityId, updates: {
        title?: string;
        content?: string;
        icon?: string | null;
        displayOrder?: number;
        savedDisplayOrder?: number | null;
        lastSeen?: number;
        completed?: boolean;
        hideCompletedChildren?: boolean;
        favorite?: boolean;
        noteType?: "document" | "task" | "tasklist";
    }, skipTimestamp?: boolean) => void;
    /** Moves a note and its sub-notes to the trash; returns the ids moved */
    readonly softDelete: (id: EARS.EntityId) => string[];
    /** Takes a note and its trashed sub-notes out of the trash; returns the ids restored */
    readonly restore: (id: EARS.EntityId) => string[];
    readonly move: (id: EARS.EntityId, newParentId: EARS.EntityId | null) => {
        oldParentId: string | null;
    };
    readonly reorder: (id: EARS.EntityId, newParentId: EARS.EntityId | null, newIndex: number) => {
        oldParentId: string | null;
        affectedIds: string[];
    };
    readonly delete: (id: EARS.EntityId) => void;
};

    readonly children: (parentId: EARS.EntityId) => NoteDTO[];

    readonly trashedDTOs: () => NoteDTO[];

    readonly connectedData: () => {

    };
};

/**
 * Open thread chat and refresh recent threads list
 *
 * Bundles:
 * - Mark thread as visited
 * - Load thread data for chat
 * - Refresh recent threads list
 */
declare function openThreadChatAndRefreshRecent(threadId: EARS.EntityId, options?: {
    restore?: boolean;
    reply?: Reply<OutgoingThreadsEvents>;
}): void;

/**
 * Open thread tab and refresh recent threads list
 *
 * Bundles:
 * - Mark thread as visited
 * - Load thread tab data with artifacts
 * - Refresh recent threads list
 */
declare function openThreadTabAndRefresh(threadId: EARS.EntityId, options?: {
    reply?: Reply<OutgoingThreadsEvents>;
}): void;

declare function paginatedMessages(threadId: EARS.EntityId, cursor?: string | null): {
    messages: Partial<MessageEntity>[];
    hasMore: boolean;
    nextCursor: string | null;
};

/**
 * Prompts Repository: the SDK's prompt repository (`promptRepository`), which owns their reads and writes, as the
 * prompts surface uses it, and the surface's views. The SDK's methods are taken as they are, not wrapped.
 */
declare const promptQueries: {
    byId: (id: _apackdev_sdk.EARS.EntityId) => _apackdev_sdk.PromptEntity | undefined;
    all: () => _apackdev_sdk.PromptEntity[];
    byLabel: (label: string) => _apackdev_sdk.PromptEntity | undefined;
    connectedData: (page?: number, pageSize?: number) => {
        prompts: _apackdev_sdk.PromptEntity[];
        page: number;
        totalPages: number;
        totalCount: number;
    };
};

/**
 * Resolve all message reference types (images, files, notes, threads,
 * library docs/folders) into prompt-ready content for the Claude Code CLI.
 *
 * Returns:
 * - `textPrefix`  — formatted text for non-image references, prepended to the user message
 * - `imageBlocks` — Anthropic image content blocks (base64-encoded), with text labels
 * - `addDirs`     — directories for `--add-dir` (attached file auto-reads)
 */
declare function resolveReferences(references: MessageReferences | undefined): Promise<{
    textPrefix: string;
    imageBlocks: any[];
    addDirs: string[];
}>;

/**
 * Send a message with custom blocks and emit MESSAGE_ADDED event
 * Use this for flow actions that need automatic frontend updates
 */
declare function sendBlockMessage(options: BlockMessageOptions): {
    messageId: EARS.EntityId;
};

/**
 * Create a choice interaction using blocks
 */
declare function sendChoiceBlock(options: {
    threadId: EARS.EntityId;
    text: string;
    prompt: string;
    choices: Array<{
        id: string;
        label: string;
        description?: string;
    }>;
    multiSelect?: boolean;
    allowCustom?: boolean;
    compact?: boolean;
    displayText?: string;
    skipOption?: {
        id: string;
        label: string;
    };
    forkable?: boolean;
} & AutoHideOptions): {
    messageId: EARS.EntityId;
};

/**
 * Create a question interaction — single question or multi-question wizard.
 * Single question = array with one item. Multi = step wizard in the frontend.
 * Response shape: string (single) or Record<string, string> (multi).
 */
declare function sendQuestionBlock(options: {
    threadId: EARS.EntityId;
    text: string;
    prompt: string;
    questions: Array<{
        question: string;
        header?: string;
        options: Array<{
            id: string;
            label: string;
            description?: string;
        }>;
        multiSelect?: boolean;
        allowCustom?: boolean;
    }>;
    forkable?: boolean;
} & AutoHideOptions): {
    messageId: EARS.EntityId;
};

/**
 * Send recent threads refresh to frontend
 *
 * Use this helper after any operation that affects thread ordering:
 * - Thread creation
 * - Message creation (updates lastMessageTimestamp)
 * - Thread visits (updates lastVisitedTimestamp)
 */
declare function sendRecentThreadsRefresh(): void;

/**
 * Send a system message (non-interactive aside) and emit MESSAGE_ADDED event
 */
declare function sendSystemMessage(options: {
    threadId: EARS.EntityId;
    text: string;
}): {
    messageId: EARS.EntityId;
};

declare function storeHandle(key: string, handle: CodexTurnHandle): void;

declare const terminalCommands: {
    /** Records a terminal the service started, under the id it took for it (`newId`) */
    create: (terminal: Pick<TerminalEntity, "cols" | "cwd" | "pid" | "rows" | "shell" | "title"> & {
        id: EARS.EntityId<"Terminal">;
    }) => EARS.EntityId;
    resize: (id: EARS.EntityId, cols: number, rows: number) => void;
    rename: (id: EARS.EntityId, customTitle: string) => void;
    updateCwd: (id: EARS.EntityId, cwd: string, title?: string) => void;
    updatePid: (id: EARS.EntityId, pid: number) => void;
    markClosed: (id: EARS.EntityId) => void;
    delete: (id: EARS.EntityId) => void;
};

declare const terminalQueries: {
    byId: (id: EARS.EntityId) => TerminalEntity | undefined;
    all: () => TerminalEntity[];
    active: () => TerminalEntity[];
};

declare const threadCommands: {
    /** `id` and `createdAt` keep an imported thread's own; a new thread gets fresh ones */
    readonly create: (input: ThreadCreateData & {
        id?: string;
        createdAt?: number;
    }) => {
        id: EARS.EntityId;
        shortCode: string;
        timestamp: number;
        status: string;
    };
    readonly update: (id: EARS.EntityId, updates: {
        topic?: string;
        instructions?: string;
        status?: string;
        tags?: string[];
        linkedThreads?: any[];
        lastMessageTimestamp?: number;
        lastVisitedTimestamp?: number;
        forcedMode?: ThreadEntity["forcedMode"] | null;
        context?: ThreadEntity["context"];
        archived?: boolean;
        chatState?: string;
    }) => void;
    readonly markAsVisited: (id: EARS.EntityId) => void;
    readonly linkFork: (sourceThreadId: EARS.EntityId, forkedThreadId: EARS.EntityId) => void;
    readonly forkCount: (sourceThreadId: EARS.EntityId) => number;
    readonly setParent: (parentId: EARS.EntityId, childIds: EARS.EntityId[]) => {
        reparented: string[];
        skipped: string[];
    };
    readonly delete: (id: EARS.EntityId) => void;
    /** Destroy a thread record left without data (a stale role holder); `delete` cleans up a real thread's links */
    readonly destroyStale: (id: EARS.EntityId) => void;
};

declare const threadQueries: {
    readonly byId: (id: EARS.EntityId) => ThreadEntity | undefined;
    readonly all: () => ThreadEntity[];
    readonly allUnfiltered: () => ThreadEntity[];
    /** The first thread holding the role, e.g. the onboarding birth thread */
    readonly idByRole: (role: EARS.RoleKind) => EARS.EntityId | undefined;
    readonly messages: (threadId: EARS.EntityId) => ({
        id: `${string}-${string}` & {
            readonly __entity?: "Message" | undefined;
        };
    } & Pick<MessageEntity & _apackdev_ears.BaseEntity & {
        id: `${string}-${string}` & {
            readonly __entity?: "Message" | undefined;
        };
    }, "compacted" | "isCommand" | "sender" | "text" | "timestamp">)[];
    readonly linkedThreads: (threadId: EARS.EntityId) => {
        id: `${string}-${string}` & {
            readonly __entity?: "Thread" | undefined;
        };
        topic: string;
        status: string;
        shortCode: _apackdev_ears.ShortCode<"THR">;
        timestamp: number;
        relation: "blocked_by" | "blocks" | "duplicates" | "parent_of";
    }[];
    readonly extendedData: (threadId: EARS.EntityId, include?: keyof ThreadExtendedData | (keyof ThreadExtendedData)[]) => ThreadExtendedData;
    readonly archivedThreads: () => Partial<ThreadEntity>[];
    readonly connectedData: () => ThreadConnectedData;
    /**
     * Compatibility alias for persisted action code compiled before artifact
     * helpers moved under chatCommands.
     */
    readonly findArtifactByType: typeof findArtifactByThreadAndType;
};

/**
 * Remove a named listener by its ID.
 * No-op if the ID doesn't exist.
 */
declare function unlisten(id: string): boolean;

/**
 * Stop and remove all cron jobs whose key starts with the given prefix.
 * Used to clean up all jobs for a specific flow actor.
 */
declare function unregisterByPrefix(prefix: string): void;

/**
 * Patch an existing artifact's title and/or content in place, and notify
 * the frontend so the panel re-renders with the new data.
 *
 * Used for artifacts that mutate across turns (e.g. the Claude Code session
 * card, which tracks live status/cost/turn count). The `ARTIFACT_UPDATED`
 * event mirrors `ARTIFACT_ADDED` but carries only the fields that changed.
 */
declare function updateAndNotify(artifactId: EARS.EntityId, options: UpdateArtifactOptions): void;

/**
 * Threads Service
 *
 * Provides primitives for updating thread-level state with automatic
 * frontend notification, following the same pattern as artifact service.
 */

/**
 * Update a thread's chatState and notify the frontend.
 *
 * This is the canonical service-level write for chatState. The DSL helper
 * `updateChatState()` in thread-context.ts handles the thread context side,
 * then delegates here for the thread write + emit.
 */
declare function updateChatState(threadId: EARS.EntityId, chatState: string): void;

/**
 * Update message state with any mutable fields
 * Main interface for ad hoc message state updates (text, blocks, blockResponse, responseTimestamp)
 * Automatically emits UPDATE_MESSAGE_STATE event to frontend
 *
 * @example
 * // Re-enable interactive blocks by clearing response
 * updateMessageState(messageId, {
 *   responseTimestamp: undefined,
 *   blockResponse: undefined
 * });
 *
 * @example
 * // Update message text
 * updateMessageState(messageId, {
 *   text: 'Updated message content'
 * });
 */
declare function updateMessageState(messageId: EARS.EntityId, updates: Partial<Pick<MessageEntity, 'blockResponse' | 'blocks' | 'compacted' | 'context' | 'forkable' | 'references' | 'responseTimestamp' | 'status' | 'text'>>): void;

/** Parse a Codex JSONL file into an array of entries. */
declare function viewByFile(filePath: string, opts?: {
    limit?: number;
    offset?: number;
}): Promise<any[]>;

export type { PackShapes as PackEntityShapes, PackFeatureSettings, PackGroupSettings, PackStepNodes, PackSurfaceEvents, PackSurfaceState, PackSystemEvents, Repositories, Services };
```
